$ sudo docker images
IMAGE                              ID             DISK USAGE   CONTENT SIZE   EXTRA
my-minio:latest                    ffe704f6910a        232MB         71.7MB    U
----
--

. Move to the working directory and create MinIO credentials.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ cat > credentials-velero << EOS
[default]
aws_access_key_id = minio      #MinIO-access_key_id
aws_secret_access_key = yyyyy  #MinIO-secret_access_key
EOS
----
--

. Obtain the Docker images to use. (Obtain images for hashicorp/vault, kes, and minio/mc.)
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ sudo docker pull hashicorp/vault:1.16
$ sudo docker pull quay.io/minio/kes:2024-01-11T13-09-29Z
$ sudo docker pull quay.io/minio/mc:RELEASE.2024-08-17T11-33-50Z
----
--

. Start Vault in dev mode.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ sudo docker run -d --name vault-dev \
--network kes-net \-p 8200:8200 \
-e VAULT_DEV_ROOT_TOKEN_ID=root \
hashicorp/vault:1.16 \
server -dev -dev-root-token-id=root
----
--

. Create a policy file on the host.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ cat > kes-policy.hcl<<EOS
path "kv/data/*" { capabilities = ["create","read","update","delete","list"] }
path "kv/metadata/*" { capabilities = ["list","read","delete"] }
EOS
----
--

. Copy the policy file to the Vault container.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ sudo docker cp kes-policy.hcl vault-dev:/tmp/kes-policy.hcl
----
--

. Initialize Vault. (AppRole / KV v2 / Policy)
+
※ In dev mode, state is lost on restart; re-enabling the authentication method, policy, role, and generating `ROLE_ID`/`SECRET_ID` is required upon restart.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ sudo docker exec -it vault-dev sh
/ # export VAULT_ADDR=http://127.0.0.1:8200
/ # export VAULT_TOKEN=root

/ # vault auth enable approle
Success! Enabled approle auth method at: approle/

/ # vault secrets enable -path=kv -version=2 kv
Success! Enabled the kv secrets engine at: kv/

/ # vault policy write kes-policy /tmp/kes-policy.hcl
Success! Uploaded policy: kes-policy

/ # vault write auth/approle/role/kes-role token_ttl=1h token_max_ttl=24h \
 secret_id_num_uses=0 secret_id_ttl=0 policies=kes-policy
Success! Data written to: auth/approle/role/kes-role

/ # vault read -field=role_id auth/approle/role/kes-role/role-id #★Generate RoleID (should be regenerated each time) - note it down
aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa
/ # vault write -f -field=secret_id auth/approle/role/kes-role/secret-id #★Generate SecretID (should be regenerated each time) - note it down
bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb

/ # vault secrets list -detailed
Path          Plugin       Accessor              Default TTL    Max TTL    Force No Cache    Replication    Seal Wrap    External Entropy Access    Options           Description                                                UUID                                    Version    Running Version          Running SHA256    Deprecation Status
----          ------       --------              -----------    -------    --------------    -----------    ---------    -----------------------    -------           -----------                                                ----                                    -------    ---------------          --------------    ------------------
cubbyhole/    cubbyhole    cubbyhole_98191b6a    n/a            n/a        false             local          false        false                      map[]             per-token private secret storage                           0293ba88-9ebb-cb15-258e-4eb0a0151d50    n/a        v1.16.3+builtin.vault    n/a               n/a
identity/     identity     identity_321e18f0     system         system     false             replicated     false        false                      map[]             identity store                                             abe9e6fd-45bb-8525-d4e0-8219d66b94b2    n/a        v1.16.3+builtin.vault    n/a               n/a
kv/           kv           kv_e3e2acc1           system         system     false             replicated     false        false                      map[version:2]    n/a                                                        24275e90-d5b8-938c-7416-b8c5beb7d980    n/a        v0.17.0+builtin          n/a               supported
secret/       kv           kv_5b22b880           system         system     false             replicated     false        false                      map[version:2]    key/value secret storage                                   64262dfb-2d70-581b-bba6-b32e8567926b    n/a        v0.17.0+builtin          n/a               supported
sys/          system       system_501475a0       n/a            n/a        false             replicated     true         false                      map[]             system endpoints used for control, policy and debugging    d5527805-f8b2-264e-bd31-0ec23f09ade5    n/a        v1.16.3+builtin.vault    n/a               n/a

/ # exit
----
--

. Generate a TLS self-signed certificate.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ mkdir -p certs
$ sudo docker run --rm \
-v "$PWD/certs:/certs" \
quay.io/minio/kes:2024-01-11T13-09-29Z \
identity new \
--key /certs/kes-server.key \
--cert /certs/kes-server.crt \
--ip 127.0.0.1 \
--dns localhost \
--dns kes \
kes

Your API key:

   kes:v1:xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx  #★★Note down the API Key

This is the only time it is shown. Keep it secret and secure!

Your Identity:

   yyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyy  #★★★Note down the Identity

The identity is not a secret. It can be shared. Any peer
needs this identity in order to verify your API key.

The generated TLS private key is stored at: /certs/kes-server.key
The generated TLS certificate is stored at: /certs/kes-server.crt

The identity can be computed again via:

    kes identity of kes:v1:xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx
    kes identity of /certs/kes-server.crt
----
--

. Create a kes-config.yaml file under the config directory.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ mkdir -p config
$ sudo docker run --rm quay.io/minio/kes:2024-01-11T13-09-29Z identity of "kes:v1:xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx" #★★ The API Key noted above
yyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyy
# vi config/kes-config.yaml
address: 0.0.0.0:7373

admin:
  identity: disabled

tls:
  key:  /certs/kes-server.key
  cert: /certs/kes-server.crt

policy:
  minio:
    allow:
      - /v1/key/create/*
      - /v1/key/generate/*
      - /v1/key/decrypt/*
      - /v1/key/bulk/decrypt
      - /v1/key/list/*
      - /v1/status
      - /v1/metrics
      - /v1/log/audit
      - /v1/log/error
    identities:
      - "yyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyyy" #★★★ Set the identity noted above

keystore:
  vault:
    endpoint: "http://vault-dev:8200"   # Use container name, not 127.0.0.1
    engine: "kv"                        # key name is 'engine'
    version: "v2"                       # string v2
    approle:
      engine: "approle"                 # explicit (default is approle)
      id:     "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa"  #★ The RoleID noted above
      secret: "********************************************"  #★ Recorded Secret ID
    status:
      ping: 10s
----
--

. Start the KES container with docker run.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ sudo docker run -d \
--name kes \
--network kes-net \
-p 7373:7373 \
-v "$PWD/config/kes-config.yaml:/config/kes-config.yaml:ro" \
-v "$PWD/certs/kes-server.key:/certs/kes-server.key:ro" \
-v "$PWD/certs/kes-server.crt:/certs/kes-server.crt:ro" \
quay.io/minio/kes:2024-01-11T13-09-29Z \
server --config /config/kes-config.yaml
----
--

. Start the MinIO container with docker run.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ sudo docker run -d \
--name minio \
--network kes-net \
-p 9000:9000 \
-p 9090:9090 \
-v "$PWD/minio:/data" \
-v "$PWD/certs/kes-server.crt:/kes-server.crt:ro" \
-e MINIO_ROOT_USER="minio" \
-e MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD="yyyyy" \
-e MINIO_KMS_KES_ENDPOINT="https://kes:7373" \
-e MINIO_KMS_KES_API_KEY="kes:v1:xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx" \  #★★ The API Key noted above
-e MINIO_KMS_KES_KEY_NAME="minio-backend-default-key" \
-e SSL_CERT_FILE="/kes-server.crt" \
my-minio:latest \
server /data --console-address ":9090"
----
--

. Create a bucket on the MinIO side.
+
--
.Execution Steps
[source,console]
----
$ sudo docker run -it --rm --network kes-net \
--entrypoint sh \
quay.io/minio/mc:RELEASE.2024-08-17T11-33-50Z
sh-5.1# mc alias set local http://minio:9000 minio yyyyy #Set alias
mc: Configuration written to `/root/.mc/config.json`. Please update your access credentials.
mc: Successfully created `/root/.mc/share`.
mc: Initialized share uploads `/root/.mc/share/uploads.json` file.
mc: Initialized share downloads `/root/.mc/share/downloads.json` file.
Added `local` successfully.
sh-5.1# mc mb local/test-velero-bucket01 #Create bucket
Bucket created successfully `minio/test-velero-bucket01`.
----
--
